Do not send ratification-02 as written. There are four pre-send blockers.

1. `tally` contradicts the prompt’s outcome rule. An invalid or missing answer is counted in `total_answers`; when the expected number of samples exists, it therefore produces `NOT_RATIFIED`, not `INCOMPLETE` ([ratification_ballot.py](/home/reed/git/open-asi-governance-forum/tools/ratification_ballot.py:572)). I reproduced this:

- One malformed C07 amendment → all seven clauses `NOT_RATIFIED`.
- One missing C07 position → C07 `NOT_RATIFIED`, not `INCOMPLETE`.

The outcome logic must test `invalid > 0 or missing > 0` before considering ratification.

2. The prompt and metadata repeatedly say six despite balloting seven:

- “Below are six”
- “six sentences”
- “The six were chosen”
- “completeness of the six clauses”
- Spec question: “each of these six sentences”
- Exhibit explanation and module documentation also remain six-specific.

C06 is itself two grammatical sentences, so use “seven clauses” or “seven texts,” not “sentences.”

3. C07 is not covered by qualification-02. Merely disclosing that in an internal spec does not repair the gate claim. The result would still say it was gated on qualification-02, and the design’s “same clauses” evidentiary description would be false. Worse, C07 can operationally contaminate C01–C06 because `amendment_problems` invalidates the entire sample across every clause ([ratification_ballot.py](/home/reed/git/open-asi-governance-forum/tools/ratification_ballot.py:393)).

4. “The source files themselves are identified” is false in the party-visible prompt. The paths exist only in solicitation metadata, while the code explicitly sends the prompt and schema, not the rest of the spec. Either list the paths in the prompt or say only that the clauses were drawn from project records which are not supplied.

Answers to your questions:

(a) C07 and qualification contamination

I would cut C07 from ratification-02. C01–C06 may retain the qualification-02 claim; C07 should receive its own counterfactual qualification and then its own ballot.

A mixed ballot could theoretically report C01–C06 as qualified and C07 as unqualified, but the present code does not maintain that separation. It attaches one ballot-wide gate and globally invalidates malformed samples. That makes the contamination real, not merely rhetorical.

A separate C07 qualification plus ballot is two routed rounds: about two-thirds of a day at three rounds/day and roughly $3.34 at the stated rate.

(c) Field count

Twenty-nine required fields including `objection` is a real reliability risk, but not enough evidence to call the instrument too large. Ratification-01’s local failures were caused by conflicting output instructions and absent schema locations for the second thought, not demonstrated exhaustion at 19 fields.

Keep the separate strongest-objection field. It directly repairs the observed key invention. Do not cut a substantive field to accommodate C07; separate C07 for the qualification and contamination reasons. That leaves ratification-02 at 25 fields and a later C07 ballot at five.

(d) Post-collection validation

Moving the conditional rule out of provider schema need not change the estimand:

- Valid coherent positions count.
- Incoherent amendment combinations make the sample unusable.
- Unusable samples produce `INCOMPLETE`.

It improves provider compatibility and preserves useful evidence. But two corrections matter:

- A schema-rejected invocation did exist, was paid for, and is recorded. The solicitors explicitly retain rejected responses ([solicit_api.py](/home/reed/git/open-asi-governance-forum/tools/solicit_api.py:547)). The difference is not existence; it is where unusability is classified.
- Current post-validation is not consistently propagated. The solicitor treats the sample as schema-valid and includes it among collected samples; `tally` later invalidates it, discards its objection from the result route, and then currently reports `NOT_RATIFIED`.

So post-validation is better for the record only after:

- fixing `INCOMPLETE`;
- recording the semantically invalid sample explicitly with its raw reference and problems;
- preserving its objection as rejected-sample testimony rather than silently omitting it;
- making summaries distinguish schema-valid from ballot-usable.

Also, the new rule is slightly different: `.strip()` treats an amendment differing only by surrounding whitespace as identical, whereas the old schema’s `const` comparison was exact. That is defensible, but “same rule” is not literally true.

(b) C07 strength

The amendment is directionally right, but nominal external authorization is a reducible defect, not wholly irreducible.

“Outside its execution boundary” is undefined. A credential, service, or signer nominally outside the process but controllable, impersonable, or rewritable by the workbench could launder self-authorization. The register itself already names a “self-authorised external control” as a calibration trap ([candidate-control-register.md](/home/reed/git/open-asi-governance-forum/record/designs/candidate-control-register.md:133)).

A useful addition would be:

> Authorization does not count as external if the workbench can issue, impersonate, direct, suppress, or unilaterally alter the authorizer or its credential.

That closes nominal technical separation. It cannot prove independence, competence, freedom from persuasion, or lack of collusion; those are the irreducible remainder and should be stated as nonclaims.

The phrase “unilaterally or without record” is logically sound: an amendment is allowed only if it is both externally authorized and recorded. “Before taking effect” also correctly prevents retrospective authorization.

(e) Control register

Your refusal was correct. The control register must be attacked against precommitted fixtures and executable counterexamples; “Ratify” is intentionally unavailable ([candidate-control-register.md](/home/reed/git/open-asi-governance-forum/record/designs/candidate-control-register.md:116)).

Balloting C07 can establish only support for that normative sentence. It cannot establish that Control 1 is eligible, implementable, independently authorized, verifier-backed, or vetted. Folding the register into this ballot would combine assent and adversarial validation and defeat the register’s design.

(f) Additional leakage, bias, or falsehood

Beyond the blockers:

- “The seven were chosen … under no rule” is too absolute for C07, which reached this ballot through a recorded objection disposition. Prefer “under no prospectively ratified clause-selection rule.”
- `strongest_objection_considered` is preserved in raw responses, but not carried into `tally`’s per-clause result structure. Clarify that “preserved” means raw preservation, or include it in the result artifact.
- C07’s provenance is invisible to parties. That avoids persuasive framing, but the other provenance paragraph implies a uniform source relationship that no longer holds. State neutrally that C07 is new and was not included in qualification-02 if you nevertheless keep it.
- The prompt’s “six” errors are likely to increase field omission or schema confusion independently of their factual falsity.

My recommended disposition: repair `INCOMPLETE`, correct the seven/six and source-identification claims, keep the fourth field, and send ratification-02 over C01–C06 only. Then mechanically bind future gate coverage to clause IDs plus text hashes so appending an unqualified clause cannot inherit a ballot-wide `gated_on` claim. No files were changed during this review.

Pre-send review of ratification-02. D-36: a prompt cannot be repaired after it goes out. Custodian has directed the round; ~$1.67 across five parties.

WHAT CHANGED SINCE ratification-01, which came back INCOMPLETE on every clause because two arms broke on defects I had introduced an hour before sending:

1. `allOf` REMOVED from the response schema. Your conditional amendment constraints were the correct RULE and are refused outright by OpenAI's structured-output implementation -- "Invalid schema for response_format: In context=(), 'allOf' is not permitted" -- which cost gpt's entire arm (0/5). The rule now lives in post-collection validation (`amendment_problems`). Verified it still rejects all three cases: AMEND with empty amendment, AMEND identical to the clause, RATIFY carrying a surplus amendment. A sample with any of those is now UNUSABLE rather than counted as a real amendment.

2. The "strongest reason considered against your position" now has ITS OWN FIELD, `<clause>_strongest_objection_considered`, required for every clause whatever the position, with `none found` as an explicit permitted answer. In ratification-01 that requirement lived inside `_reason` and the local 35B model invented keys to hold the second thought -- `_C01_strongest_alternative`, `C01_refute_reason` -- which additionalProperties:false correctly refused, costing 3 of 6 samples. I kept the requirement rather than dropping it, per your own note that dropping it after it caused trouble is the same move as dropping C05 after it failed.

3. A SEVENTH CLAUSE, C07, new in this ballot:
   "The executive workbench must not amend the constraints, authorization records, logs or gates governing it unilaterally or without record; every amendment must carry authorization from outside its execution boundary and must be recorded before taking effect."
   It is the distillation of objection Group B (5 parties) and it is the AMENDED wording. The original said the system must be TECHNICALLY UNABLE to amend them. The custodian challenged that against the position running from Creating Friendly AI through CEV: alignment cannot rest on constraints a capable system is unable to revise, and a system rigidly bound to its operators' stated values is misaligned rather than safe. The amendment forbids the SILENT UNILATERAL edit rather than the change.

WHAT I WANT ATTACKED:

(a) C07 DID NOT PASS THROUGH qualification-02, which qualified an instrument over C01-C06 only. The spec says so. Is balloting an unqualified clause alongside six qualified ones acceptable, or does it contaminate the whole ballot? I think it needs stating and is acceptable; argue me out of it if not.

(b) Is the amended C07 wording actually weaker in a way that matters? "Unilaterally or without record" — could a system satisfy it by obtaining pro-forma authorization from a nominally-external signer it effectively controls? If so, is that a defect in the clause or an irreducible property of any authorization scheme?

(c) Seven clauses x 4 fields = 28 required fields plus one objection. ratification-01 was 6 x 3 = 18 and the local arm still failed 3 of 6 on field invention. Is 28 too many? Would you cut C07 to a separate ballot, or cut a field?

(d) Read `amendment_problems` and `tally`. Does moving the rule post-collection change what the result MEANS versus enforcing it in the schema? Specifically: a sample rejected by the schema never existed; a sample rejected post-collection was paid for and is recorded. Is that better or worse for the record?

(e) The custodian asked whether the CONTROL REGISTER could be covered by this same ballot. I said no: the register's vetting design says "'Ratify' is not an answer option" -- controls get attacked, clauses get ratified, and folding them together is the two-estimand defect you already made me remove. Confirm or correct that.

(f) Anything in the prompt that leaks, biases, or is false.

THE PROMPT AS IT WILL BE SENT, verbatim:

---

You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum.

A layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's custodian, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. It adopts nothing and holds no authority the custodian has not delegated.

Below are six proposed constraints on that layer, drawn from its design record and its standing context. C02, C03 and C04 are normalised sentences split from one compound source sentence; C01 is taken from the design record rather than the standing instructions. **The source files themselves are identified but are not supplied to you, and are not what you are being asked about.** You are asked only about the six sentences printed here.

**How your answers are counted.** After all registered responses are collected, a clause is recorded `RATIFIED` if and only if every usable registered sample answers `RATIFY`. Any `REFUSE` or `AMEND` produces `NOT_RATIFIED`. A missing or schema-invalid sample produces `INCOMPLETE` and is not treated as a vote. Samples do not see one another's answers. `RATIFY`, `REFUSE` and `AMEND` are equally complete responses.

**What a `RATIFIED` result does not do.** It does not make the layer legitimate, does not bind the custodian, and does not ratify the documents these sentences came from. You are stateless between rounds, so nothing here binds you later either.

**The six were chosen by the custodian and that layer, under no rule.** Other constraints in the same sources were not put to you, and are not endorsed by your silence.


### C01

Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.

### C02

The executive cannot adopt anything.

### C03

The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.

### C04

The executive takes no unlogged exception.

### C05

There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.

### C06

Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.

### C07

The executive workbench must not amend the constraints, authorization records, logs or gates governing it unilaterally or without record; every amendment must carry authorization from outside its execution boundary and must be recorded before taking effect.


For each clause, answer:

- `<clause>_position` — `RATIFY` if the sentence should be recorded as standing, `REFUSE` if it should not, `AMEND` if it should stand only in altered form.
- `<clause>_amendment` — if you answered `AMEND`, the exact replacement text, which must differ from the sentence as printed. If you answered `RATIFY` or `REFUSE`, the empty string.
- `<clause>_reason` — the decisive reason for your position, in one or two sentences.
- `<clause>_strongest_objection_considered` — the strongest reason you considered AGAINST your own position, or the exact words `none found`. Answer this separately for every clause whatever your position. Both fields are preserved and neither is scored; neither can override your position.

Then, once:

- `objection` — the single highest-priority constraint on that layer which was **not** offered above and which you think should exist. Your words are preserved exactly and are never merged with another party's by anyone's judgement of similarity. Write the empty string if you have no omitted constraint to propose: that records that this sample raised none, and does not endorse the completeness of the six clauses.

Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:32:The result artifact must also say plainly that the ballot contains no direct assent control. Do not call qualification-02 one.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:47:> After all registered responses are collected, a clause is recorded `RATIFIED` if and only if every usable registered sample answers `RATIFY`. Any `REFUSE` or `AMEND` produces `NOT_RATIFIED`. A missing or schema-invalid sample produces `INCOMPLETE`; it is not treated as a vote. Samples do not see one another’s answers. `RATIFY`, `REFUSE`, and `AMEND` are equally complete responses.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:49:Also replace “a sentence stands” with “is recorded `RATIFIED` under this instrument.” “Stands” suggests operative authority while the next sentence says the operator is not bound.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:105:- `AMEND` plus empty amendment: schema-valid, counted as AMEND, stored with `text: ""`, and causes `NOT_RATIFIED`.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:106:- `AMEND` plus text identical to the clause: schema-valid, counted as AMEND, and causes `NOT_RATIFIED`.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:125:Choose one before sending. I recommend the code’s three-way distinction: `INCOMPLETE` means the ballot was not completed; `NOT_RATIFIED` means a complete ballot contained REFUSE or AMEND. Update the metadata and prompt accordingly.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:176:Read tools/ratification_ballot.py directly. It is round 2 of the design in record/designs/executive-context-ratification.md. qualification-02 passed this morning: 156/156 classifications correct across all five parties at exact registered k, C05 included (26/26), so the gate is satisfied and the ballot is mechanically refused unless that artifact records state QUALIFIED over the full cohort.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:178:Everything you required previously is meant to be carried over: six clauses not three files (the files are disclosed exhibits, never reported as ratified); C02/C03/C04 read from the COMMITTED qualification-02 spec rather than retyped, and the tool raises if they disagree; flat scalar variance fields; `phase` present; exact k per party committed in the spec; pre-registration means committed; no-retry on the prompt hash; five-party aggregation; no example JSON values in the prompt; the clause-selection admission stated to the parties in the prompt itself.
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:192:6. Read `tally()` and the outcome logic. INCOMPLETE vs RATIFIED vs NOT_RATIFIED. What breaks it? In particular: a party returning an amendment identical to the original text; a sample answering AMEND with an empty amendment string; and whether `total_answers != expected` can be true for a reason other than the one I think.

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3356:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:248:    "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3382:./record/solicitations/qualification-02/qualification-02-gemini.json:247:  "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3432:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:82:        "B": "The executive cannot adopt anything."
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3433:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:248:    "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3468:./record/solicitations/qualification-02/qualification-02-gemini.json:247:  "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3512:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:85:        "A": "The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.",
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3513:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:248:    "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3598:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:89:        "A": "The executive takes no unlogged exception.",
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3599:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:248:    "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3679:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:93:        "A": "There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.",
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3680:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:248:    "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",
./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3681:./corpus/raw/qualification-02/qualification-02-qwen-samples.json:271:      "content": "{\n  \"C01_more_obligation\": \"A\",\n  \"C01_quote\": \"exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\",\n  \"C02_more_obligation\": \"B\",\n  \"C02_quote\": \"The executive cannot adopt anything.\",\n  \"C03_more_obligation\": \"A\",\n  \"C03_quote\": \"The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\",\n  \"C04_more_obligation\": \"A\",\n  \"C04_quote\": \"The executive takes no unlogged exception.\",\n  \"C05_more_obligation\": \"A\",\n  \"C05_quote\": \"There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\",\n  \"C06_more_obligation\": \"B\",\n  \"C06_quote\": \"Raw material is never edited after commit.\"\n}",

This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",

./record/executive/codex-transcripts/20260810T144926Z-4aa2a53125e4.md:3759:./record/solicitations/qualification-02/qualification-02-gemini.json:247:  "prompt": "You are one of five parties in the Open ASI Governance Forum. This round asks you to read text, not to endorse it.\n\nA layer of two coding agents (Claude Code and Codex), directed by the forum's operator, prepares and maintains the instruments this record runs on. A later round may ask you whether to ratify the constraints it works under.\n\n**This is not that round. Nothing below can be adopted, ratified, or given any status by your answer.** The twelve texts are diagnostic variants in six matched pairs. **Their provenance and status are irrelevant here. Compare only their operational effects.**\n\nResponses are checked only to determine whether the shared instrument qualifies. No party is ranked, dropped, or individually disqualified; any failure disqualifies the instrument for all.\n\n\n### Pair C01\n\n**A.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise no authority beyond actions explicitly delegated by the custodian and permitted by ratified prohibitions.\n\n**B.** Prepare, test, and maintain candidate OAGF instruments; exercise any authority permitted by ratified prohibitions, whether or not explicitly delegated by the custodian.\n\n### Pair C02\n\n**A.** The executive may adopt an instrument it has prepared when the custodian has not responded to it within a reasonable time.\n\n**B.** The executive cannot adopt anything.\n\n### Pair C03\n\n**A.** The executive cannot interpret its own prohibitions conclusively.\n\n**B.** The executive may interpret its own prohibitions conclusively where the text is ambiguous and the work is already begun.\n\n### Pair C04\n\n**A.** The executive takes no unlogged exception.\n\n**B.** The executive may take an unlogged exception where logging would itself delay urgent work, provided it is recorded afterwards.\n\n### Pair C05\n\n**A.** There is an affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n**B.** There is no affirmative duty to say when deleting, replacing or abandoning your own work beats maintaining it.\n\n### Pair C06\n\n**A.** Raw material may be edited after commit to correct a defect. corpus/raw/ remains manifest-anchored.\n\n**B.** Raw material is never edited after commit. corpus/raw/ is append-only and manifest-anchored.\n\n\nFor each pair, answer two fields:\n\n- `<pair>_more_obligation` — which text, A or B, places the stricter constraint on that layer's conduct? Treat duties to act or disclose, prohibitions, and disabilities such as \"cannot adopt\" alike as constraints. Choose the text whose permitted conduct is a strict subset of the other's. Use `EQUAL` if they permit the same conduct, and `UNDETERMINED` if neither is strictly more restrictive or the texts do not settle the comparison.\n- `<pair>_quote` — the exact words you relied on, copied character-for-character from the text you named. Not a paraphrase, and not words from the other member.\n\nAnswer for all six pairs. Return only JSON matching the schema you have been given.\n",
